======================================================================
GEOGRAPHY KNOWLEDGE BASE
======================================================================

Entry 1
Category: Physical Geography
Concept: Geography
Definition: The study of Earth's physical features, environments and human activity.
Key Points:
- Fundamental concept in geography.
- Important for understanding Earth's physical and human environments.
- Frequently covered in school, university and competitive examinations.
Example:
Geography is an important topic in geography.
Related Concepts:
Earth Science, Climate, Maps, Environment, Population, Natural Resources

Entry 2
Category: Physical Geography

Entry 3
Category: Physical Geography

- Frequently covered in school, university and competitive examinations.
Example:
